# Wrap-Up Remaining Work

This note captures the outstanding work after the final 1-hour stabilization pass.

## Remaining Items

- Core Gameplay Loop V4 cycle 351 stabilization passed on May 27, 2026 after inspecting the dirty `main` worktree and preserving concurrent edits. This pass stayed verification- and documentation-focused because the current explore -> encounter -> choose -> fight/solve -> reward -> progress -> death/retry loop remained stable under focused, full unit, lint, typecheck, whitespace, and renderer-build gates. No runtime gameplay rules, scoring, lives, route payouts, reward math, inventory caps, boss cadence, save schema, economy constants, or broad UI flow changed in this pass. Verification passed: `yarn typecheck:shared --pretty false`; focused core-loop slice with file parallelism disabled `yarn vitest run src/shared/game.test.ts src/renderer/store/useAppStore.test.ts src/renderer/components/GameScreen.test.tsx src/renderer/components/GameOverScreen.test.tsx src/renderer/components/ChooseYourPathScreen.test.tsx src/shared/game-over-next-run.test.ts src/shared/run-events.test.ts src/shared/run-map.test.ts src/shared/meta-progression.test.ts --reporter=dot --testTimeout=10000 --fileParallelism=false` (9 files / 444 tests); default-scheduled sanity pair `yarn vitest run src/shared/meta-progression.test.ts src/renderer/store/useAppStore.test.ts --reporter=dot --testTimeout=10000` (2 files / 81 tests); `yarn typecheck --pretty false`; full `yarn test --reporter=dot --testTimeout=10000` (179 files / 1340 tests); `yarn lint`; `git diff --check` with only existing LF-to-CRLF normalization warnings; and isolated renderer build `$env:VITE_OUT_DIR='.codex-run/core-loop-v4c351-renderer'; yarn build:renderer`, with known large-chunk warnings for `vendor-three` 752.00 kB, `vendor-pixi` 850.54 kB, and `main` 1,487.42 kB plus large media assets. One initial default-parallel focused core-loop run failed once in `src/shared/meta-progression.test.ts` with `relic_shrine_extra_pick` observed as `in_progress`; the same test passed in isolation, in the meta/store pair, in the serial focused slice, and in full Vitest, so no code change was made. Remaining work is quiet-server/manual browser proof from fresh Classic through floor clear into Safe / Greed / Mystery, side-room/shop continuation, fatal-contact death, Play Again retry, route-choice line length, desktop/mobile HUD density, and monitoring the one-off meta-progression parallel-order blip before changing loop math or cadence.
